  89:         private fun startCountdown(audioGranted: Boolean) {
  90:             val osPermissionGranted =
  91:                 ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO) ==
  92:                     PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
  93:             if (audioGranted && !osPermissionGranted) {
  94:                 // Pause and ask UI to request the OS permission; countdown resumes via onAudioPermissionResult.
  95:                 _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.RequestAudioPermission
  96:                 return
  97:             }
  98:             countdownJob?.cancel()
  99:             countdownJob =
 100:                 viewModelScope.launch {
 101:                     if (audioGranted && osPermissionGranted) {
 102:                         // Wipe any stale file (crash leftover, old build) before this recording starts.
 103:                         wipeStaleSosFile()
 104:                         startRecording()
 105:                     }
 106:                     for (sec in 30 downTo 1) {
 107:                         _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.Countdown(sec)
 108:                         delay(1_000L)
 109:                     }
 110:                     stopRecording()
 111:                     fireSos()
 112:                 }
 113:         }
 114: 
 115:         private fun startRecording() {
 116:             runCatching {
 117:                 val dir = File(context.filesDir, "sos").also { it.mkdirs() }
 118:                 val file = File(dir, "sos-$bookingId.m4a")
 119:                 val rec =
 120:                     if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
 121:                         MediaRecorder(context)
 122:                     } else {
 123:                         @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
 124:                         MediaRecorder()
 125:                     }
 126:                 rec.apply {
 127:                     setAudioSource(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC)
 128:                     setOutputFormat(MediaRecorder.OutputFormat.MPEG_4)
 129:                     setAudioEncoder(MediaRecorder.AudioEncoder.AAC)
 130:                     setOutputFile(file.absolutePath)
 131:                     prepare()
 132:                     start()
 133:                 }
 134:                 recorder = rec
 135:             }
 136:         }
 137: 
 138:         private fun stopRecording() {
 139:             runCatching {
 140:                 recorder?.apply {
 141:                     stop()
 142:                     release()
 143:                 }
 144:                 recorder = null
 145:             }
 146:         }
 147: 
 148:         private suspend fun fireSos() {
 149:             val result = sosUseCase.execute(bookingId)
 150:             if (!result.isSuccess) {
 151:                 _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.SosError(result.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Unknown error")
 152:                 return
 153:             }
 154:             _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.SosConfirmed
 155:             maybeUploadEvidence()
 156:         }
 157: 
 158:         private suspend fun maybeUploadEvidence() {
 159:             if (!featureFlags.sosAudioUploadEnabled()) return
 160:             val customerId = (sessionManager.authState.value as? AuthState.Authenticated)?.uid
 161:             val file = File(File(context.filesDir, "sos"), "sos-$bookingId.m4a")
 162:             if (customerId == null || !file.exists()) return
 163: 
 164:             val bytes = file.readBytes()
 165:             file.delete()
 166:             audioUploader.upload(customerId, bookingId, bytes).collect { progress ->
 167:                 _sosUiState.value =
 168:                     when (progress) {
 169:                         is SosUploadProgress.Progress -> SosUiState.UploadingEvidence(progress.pct)
 170:                         is SosUploadProgress.Success -> SosUiState.EvidenceSaved
 171:                         is SosUploadProgress.Failure ->
 172:                             SosUiState.EvidenceUploadError(progress.cause.message ?: "upload_failed")
 173:                     }
 174:             }
 175:         }
 176: 
 177:         private fun wipeStaleSosFile() {
 178:             runCatching { File(File(context.filesDir, "sos"), "sos-$bookingId.m4a").delete() }
 179:         }

The upload flow can send stale local audio even when the current SOS flow has no consent or recording, which is a privacy-sensitive functional bug. The patch should not be considered correct until the upload is tied to a fresh recording or stale files are cleared before opt-out paths.

Review comment:

- [P1] Do not upload stale SOS audio after opt-out — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E11-S05b-2\customer-app\app\src\main\kotlin\com\homeservices\customer\ui\tracking\SosViewModel.kt:161-164
  When `customer.sos-audio-upload.enabled` is on and a stale `sos-$bookingId.m4a` exists from a crash/old build/previous recording, a user who denies audio consent or OS permission never records or wipes that file, but `maybeUploadEvidence` still reads and uploads any existing file. This can upload audio after the user opted out; wipe stale files before non-recording countdowns or only upload a file recorded in the current SOS flow.

The upload flow can send stale local audio even when the current SOS flow has no consent or recording, which is a privacy-sensitive functional bug. The patch should not be considered correct until the upload is tied to a fresh recording or stale files are cleared before opt-out paths.

Review comment:

- [P1] Do not upload stale SOS audio after opt-out — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E11-S05b-2\customer-app\app\src\main\kotlin\com\homeservices\customer\ui\tracking\SosViewModel.kt:161-164
  When `customer.sos-audio-upload.enabled` is on and a stale `sos-$bookingId.m4a` exists from a crash/old build/previous recording, a user who denies audio consent or OS permission never records or wipes that file, but `maybeUploadEvidence` still reads and uploads any existing file. This can upload audio after the user opted out; wipe stale files before non-recording countdowns or only upload a file recorded in the current SOS flow.
